Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the neuro object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 283. The moduli space has dimension at most 644 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on neuro structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 283. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 644. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on neuro structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 669. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 333 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 669 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the neuro structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 333. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
